## 3. About us (Who We Are)

*Source: https://www.pink-knots.com/profile.html — copied verbatim.*

Since the inception in the year 2015, "Pink Knots", is successfully manufacturing and supplying the decoration needs of the customers with Wooden Box And Trays. We are counted as a prominent manufacturer and supplier of a huge collection of Metal Bird Houses, Wooden Cutlery Boxes and Wooden Candle Holders. Exhibiting traditional and modern looks, our items are offered in an ambit of designs, color combinations, patterns, postures and sizes so as to meet diverse preferences of customers. With fine finishing and unmatched styles, the range is ideal to add beauty to every place. Gradually, we are able to cover every nook and corner and have become the prominent choice in the market.

We have invested a huge amount in developing a state-of-the-art infrastructure that spreads over an extensive area. We have divided the entire structure into several departments such the production unit, quality control, warehouse & packaging and administrative sections. We are having support of such creative and sincere personnel who employ their creativity in developing traditional designed crafts. Our artisans keep themselves abreast of modern designs and strive to carve offered product range in a perfect amalgamation of traditional & prevailing designs. We have appointed a team of quality inspectors, who perform the entire quality tests under some strict parameters. Further, we pay attention towards packaging offered handicraft items in such a way which ensure their safe loading-unloading and transits.

Within short span, we have been successful in establishing our strong presence in the domestic market. Under the competent directorship of "Ms. Nidhi", our organization has been scaling the new heights of success. Constant motivation and guidance of our leader is the main reason behind the tremendous growth of our business.

> ⚠️ The About text is generic IndiaMART boilerplate: it mentions "Metal Bird Houses" and "Wooden Candle Holders" (not in the catalogue) and names "Ms. Nidhi" as director, while the factsheet names Arushi Kathuria as CEO/founder. Rewrite before reuse.

## 5. Team, infrastructure, quality & why us

*Source: profile.html — verbatim.*

### Our Team

We have an efficient and skilled team of professionals that possesses vast expertise in the field of decorative items. The entire credit of our huge growth and success in this industry goes to the sincere and hard work of our team. The skills of the team enable us to offer the best range of products with complete satisfaction. We have selected every single team member after reviewing their knowledge, expertise and talent in this industry domain.

Our team includes:

- Procurement agents
- Production professionals
- Quality controllers
- Warehousing experts
- Packaging personnel
- Sales & marketing staff

### Our Infrastructure

For having a roof, where all the business operations of our firm take place, we have developed a sound infrastructure. It includes variegated specific wings like of carving, polishing, storage and packaging, under a same roof to ensure efficient as well as coordinated working. All the detailing related tasks of handicraft items take place at production set up. Moreover, for enhancing appeal and ensuring safe transits, we pack and provide these to clients in qualitative packaging, which is done at a specific wing.

### Quality Assurance

Right from the day of our foundation, fulfilling clients' demand with attractive collection of decorative items has been our priority. Due to this, quality assured material is used by our artisans for making best quality products. Each stage of carving, engraving and other production process is supervised by experienced quality controllers. Moreover, manufactured craft items are scrutinized by these professionals on the basis of their looks, design accuracy and finishing.

### Why Us?

Following are the distinguishing factors that have helped us to reach great heights:

- Remarkable product-line
- Sound infrastructure set up
- Prompt delivery of orders
- Excellent financial position
- Stringent quality tests before sourcing the range
- Experienced team
- Wide distribution network
- Ethical business deals

| Printing | Digital |
| Country of Origin | Made in India |
| shape | customized (Square, Rectangle, Round) |
| Brand | Pink Knots |

Kappa board boxes are known for their durability, stackability, and ease of handling. They are designed to withstand rough handling, compression, and stacking without compromising the contents inside. The fluted layer provides cushioning and shock absorption, minimizing the risk of damage during transportation.

These boxes are also environmentally friendly as they are usually made from recycled paper materials and can be recycled themselves after use. The lightweight nature of kappa board helps reduce shipping costs and energy consumption during transportation.

Facial tissue Or Paper Handkerchief refers to a class of soft, absorbent, disposable papers that are suitable for use on the face. They are disposable alternatives for cloth handkerchiefs. Our Facial tissue and paper handkerchiefs are made from the lowest basis weights tissue paper (14 18 g/m2). The surface is often made smoother by light calendaring. These paper types consist usually of 2–3 plies. Because of high quality requirements the base tissue is normally made entirely from pure chemical pulp, but might contain added selected recycled fiber. The tissue paper might be treated with softeners, lotions or added perfume to get the right properties or "feeling". The finished facial tissues or handkerchiefs are folded and put in pocket-size packages or a box dispenser.

## 11. Notes for the rebuild

- **Prices:** 404 of 408 listings show a price, ranging from ₹20 to ₹5,000. Four listings say "Ask for price" and have no details: Handicraft Basket, Jewelry Packaging Box, Wedding Gift and Dry Fruit Boxes.
- **Repeated product names:** 38 names appear more than once (in several categories, or twice within one), for example Canvas Tote Bag, Cardboard Packaging Box, Corporate Gift Box, Corporate Tea Gift Box, Custom Wooden Serving Platter, Customized Cardboard Box, Customized Wooden Serving Trays, Decorative Wooden Trays. Merge or rename these when restructuring.
- **Category overlap:** several categories cover the same ground (e.g. "Wooden Box", "Wooden Box And Wooden Tray", "Corporate Gift Wooden Box", "Wooden Gift Boxes For Diwali"; "Jewelry Box", "Earring Jewelry Box", "Plastic Jewelry Box"). A rebuild could group them under a few top-level collections: Wedding, Festive & Diwali, Corporate Gifting, Trays & Serveware, Boxes & Packaging, Home & Office Accessories.
- **Copy quality:** the About, Infrastructure and Quality sections are IndiaMART boilerplate that mentions carving and handicrafts. Product descriptions are short and uneven. Everything needs a brand-voice rewrite.
- **Common specs:** materials are mostly wood/MDF and cardboard/paper, with some leather/leatherette. Most items are digitally printed (CMYK), customisable in size, logo and design, and "Made in India". The usual minimum order is 50 pieces (on 298 listings; 100 pieces on 36). Delivery is usually 2–3 weeks.
- **Contact:** the site has no direct email or brand social profiles, and the phone number is an IndiaMART routing number. Get direct contact details from the client.
